38. Taiwan Today -  (Taiwan to establish 1st white dolphin reserve) 2014-04-22 Past Stories   


-----------------------------------------------------------------------------








文字快照 :http://taiwantoday.tw/ct.asp?xItem=216689&CtNode=419




...

Taiwan will establish its first marine wildlife sanctuary to protect the Chinese white dolphin, or Sousa chinensis, the Forestry Bureau under the ROC Council of Agriculture said April 21.

Establishment of the reserve in 763 square kilometers of ocean between Longfeng fishing port in Miaoli County, central Taiwan, and Waisanding Sand Bar Lighthouse in Chiayi County to the south, will place a reasonable restriction on coastal development and fishing operations, the bureau said. It can give appropriate protection to the intertidal zone and help nurture local fish stocks, benefitting dolphins and local fishermen.

The Chinese white dolphin’s range stretches from the south of the Yangtze in mainland China to the east coast of Africa, including seas off Australia’s north coast and Taiwan’s...
